while read -r SWITCH SWITCH_IP
do
  echo
  echo "===== SWITCH: $SWITCH ($SWITCH_IP) ====="

  if ssh -tt \
    -o ConnectTimeout=10 \
    -o ServerAliveInterval=15 \
    -o ServerAliveCountMax=3 \
    "cumulus@$SWITCH_IP" '
      CHECK_RC=0

      echo "===== SWITCH IDENTITY ====="
      hostname

      echo "===== ROCE PROFILE ====="
      nv show qos roce || CHECK_RC=1

      echo "===== PENDING CONFIGURATION ====="
      nv config diff || CHECK_RC=1

      echo "===== INTERFACE STATUS ====="
      nv show interface || CHECK_RC=1

      echo "===== BGP SUMMARY ====="
      sudo vtysh -c "show bgp ipv4 unicast summary" || CHECK_RC=1

      exit "$CHECK_RC"
    ' 2>&1 | tee "$REPORT_DIR/${SWITCH}_check.log"
  then
    printf '%s\t%s\tCOLLECTED_REVIEW_REPORT\n' \
      "$SWITCH" "$SWITCH_IP" >> "$SUMMARY"
  else
    printf '%s\t%s\tCOLLECTION_FAILED_OR_INCOMPLETE\n' \
      "$SWITCH" "$SWITCH_IP" >> "$SUMMARY"
  fi
done <<'SWITCHES'
spine-01 172.31.17.5
spine-02 172.31.17.18
spine-03 172.31.17.20
spine-04 172.31.17.22
leaf-01 172.31.17.19
leaf-02 172.31.17.21
leaf-03 172.31.17.23
leaf-04 172.31.17.24
SWITCHES

echo
echo "===== COLLECTION SUMMARY ====="
cat "$SUMMARY"

echo
echo "===== CLIENT ASSESSMENTS ====="

  'Overall assessment:|PASS:.*WARN:.*FAIL:|ERROR:' \
  "$REPORT_DIR"/weka*_precheck.log || true

echo "Reports saved to: $REPORT_DIR"
echo "Collection results are not an overall readiness verdict."
echo "No network settings were changed and no hosts were rebooted."
